function koItemBasketError102(inv,qty){

  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(value)||value<0||value>koItemLimit102(id))return `${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[id]}의 수량을 확인해 주세요.`;

  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(stock)||stock<0||value>0&&stock+value>koItemLimit102(id))return `${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[id]}은(는) 보유 상한 ${koItemLimit102(id)}개를 넘습니다.`;
 }
 const traits=Object.entries(inv??{}).filter(([id])=>id.startsWith('trait:')).reduce((n,[,v])=>n+(Number.isSafeInteger(v)&&v>0?v:0),0);
 const adding=Object.entries(qty).filter(([id])=>id.startsWith('trait:')).reduce((n,[,v])=>n+v,0);
 return adding>0&&traits+adding>10?'성격개변최면제는 서로 다른 종류를 합해 10개까지 보관할 수 있습니다.':null;
}

function koApplyGuest299(guest,state){
 const template=koGuestTemplate299(guest.name);if(!template)return guest;
 const p=koProfile299(template),lo=Math.min(p.walletMin,p.walletMax),hi=Math.max(p.walletMin,p.walletMax);
 const studio=koStudio299(koGuestMod299());
 if(Object.hasOwn(studio.overrides,guest.name)&&!Object.hasOwn(studio.baselines,guest.name)){
  // A normal record created while an override is active can be restored without
  // erasing visit/relationship history. The dispatch commits this to modData
  // atomically; in-place edits would corrupt undo snapshots.
  koPendingGuestBaselines299[guest.name]??=koProfile299(state?.patronRecords?.find(x=>x.name===guest.name)??guest);
 }
 return {...guest,patronType:p.patronType,intent:p.intent,tacticStyle:p.tacticStyle,boldness:p.boldness,charm:p.charm,chemistry:p.chemistry,walletGold:lo+Math.floor(Math.random()*(hi-lo+1)),attributes:{...p.attributes},personaTags:[...p.personaTags]};
}

function koGrandmotherHired299(state){
 // Legacy 2.99 saves have only `day`. A previous-day hire belongs to this
 // shift only before the next preparation period. New hires expire on close.
 return koGuestMod299()?.employees?.some(x=>x?.kind==='grandmother'&&(x.untilClose===true||x.day===state.day||(x.day===state.day-1&&state.slot%Eo<OE&&['service','last_call'].includes(state.phase))));
}
function koWorker299(state){
 const hired=koGrandmotherHired299(state);
 if(!hired||!['service','last_call'].includes(state.phase)||state.slot%5!==0)return state;
 if(koGrandmaWatching299(state))return state;
 const scene=B(state),busy=new Set(scene?[scene.customerId,...scene.crowdPatronIds??[],...scene.joinedPatronIds??[]]:[]);
 const available=state.customers.filter(x=>_t(state,x.id)==='taproom'&&!busy.has(x.id)&&!['gone','leaving'].includes(x.status));
 const serving=available.find(x=>x.status==='ordered'&&x.order&&Lc(state,x.order));
 if(serving){const order=serving.order,cost=Y$(order),supplied=F$(state,order);return Mt({...supplied,customers:supplied.customers.map(x=>x.id===serving.id?{...x,status:'drinking',tab:x.tab+cost,walletGold:Math.max(0,x.walletGold-cost),servedBy:'grandmother',stayTimer:20+Math.floor(Math.random()*40)}:x),customersServedToday:supplied.customersServedToday+(serving.roundsOrdered===1?1:0),log:[...supplied.log,{slot:supplied.slot,text:`할머니 종업원이 ${serving.name}에게 ${m.koLabel('order',order)}을(를) 내온다. (+${cost}골드)`}]},'service',cost)}
 const orderable=available.find(x=>x.status==='greeted'||x.status==='wants_more');
 if(orderable){const order=wI();return{...state,customers:state.customers.map(x=>x.id===orderable.id?{...x,status:'ordered',order,roundsOrdered:x.roundsOrdered+1,patience:x.maxPatience}:x),log:[...state.log,{slot:state.slot,text:`할머니 종업원이 ${orderable.name}의 주문을 받는다: ${m.koLabel('order',order)}.`}]}}
 const arrived=available.find(x=>x.status==='arrived');
 if(arrived)return{...state,customers:state.customers.map(x=>x.id===arrived.id?{...x,status:'greeted'}:x),log:[...state.log,{slot:state.slot,text:`할머니 종업원이 ${arrived.name}을(를) 맞이하고 자리로 안내한다.`}]};
 if(state.tavernCleanliness<70)return{...state,tavernCleanliness:Math.min(100,state.tavernCleanliness+5),log:[...state.log,{slot:state.slot,text:'할머니 종업원이 홀의 테이블을 닦는다.'}]};
 return state;
}
function koShopAction299(state,action){
 if(state.gameOver||state.pendingEvent||state.errandSession||state.activeShoppingSession||Qt(state)||Ge(state)||B(state)?.playerEngaged)return state;
 const qty=action.qty??{},valid=(ids,max)=>ids.every(id=>Number.isSafeInteger(qty[id]??0)&&(qty[id]??0)>=0&&(qty[id]??0)<=max)&&ids.some(id=>qty[id]>0);
 if(action.type==='ko299_delivery'){
  if(!valid(['food','beer','wine'],99))return state;
  const cost=Math.ceil(1.25*(['food','beer','wine'].reduce((n,id)=>n+(qty[id]??0)*om(state,id),0)));
  if(cost>state.economy.gold)return state;
  return D({...state,economy:{...state.economy,gold:state.economy.gold-cost},supplies:{...state.supplies,food:state.supplies.food+(qty.food??0),beer:state.supplies.beer+(qty.beer??0),wine:state.supplies.wine+(qty.wine??0)}},`긴급 배달로 식재료 ${qty.food??0}, 맥주 ${qty.beer??0}, 와인 ${qty.wine??0}을(를) 받았다. 시세와 배달료를 포함해 ${cost}골드를 지불했다.`)
 }
 if(action.type==='ko299_trip'){
  if(state.phase!=='prep'||(state.player.stats.energy??0)<10||Qt(state)||B(state)||Ge(state))return state;
  if(koItemBasketError102(koGuestMod299()?.inventory,qty))return state;
  const cost=Object.entries(qty).reduce((n,[id,v])=>n+koItemPrice102(id)*v,0);
  if(cost>state.economy.gold)return state;
  const summary=Object.entries(qty).filter(([,v])=>v>0).map(([id,v])=>`${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[id]} ${v}`).join(', ');
  let next=D({...state,economy:{...state.economy,gold:state.economy.gold-cost}},`당신은 시장에 가서 ${summary}을(를) ${cost}골드에 샀다. 왕복에는 한 시간이 걸린다.`);
  const old=next.awayEvents?.length??0;
  for(let i=0;i<60&&!next.gameOver;i++)next=Ol(next,{playerPresent:false});
  if((next.awayEvents?.length??0)>old){const later=next.awayEvents.slice(old).map(x=>({slot:x.slot,text:`(당신이 외출한 동안) ${x.text}`,seq:x.seq}));next={...next,log:[...next.log,...later],awayEvents:next.awayEvents.slice(0,old)}}
  return next;
 }
 if(action.type==='ko299_hire'){
  if(state.phase!=='prep'||state.economy.gold<10||koGrandmotherHired299(state))return state;
  return D({...state,economy:{...state.economy,gold:state.economy.gold-10}},'할머니 종업원을 오늘 영업에 10골드로 고용했다. 개점부터 문을 닫을 때까지 홀을 거든다.')
 }
 if(action.type==='ko299_use'){
  const item=action.item,target=action.target,inv=koGuestMod299()?.inventory;
  if(!Object.hasOwn(KO299_ITEM_NAMES,item)||!['wife','player'].includes(target)||!(inv?.[item]>0))return state;
  const who=target==='wife'?g(state):state.player;
  if(item==='energy'){
   const old=who.stats?.energy;if(!Number.isFinite(old)||old>=100)return state;
   const now=Math.min(100,old+10),updated={...who,stats:{...who.stats,energy:now}};
   return D(target==='wife'?{...state,characters:{...state.characters,[state.primaryCharacterId]:updated}}:{...state,player:updated},`${target==='wife'?who.name:'당신'}이(가) ${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[item]}을(를) 마셨다. 기력 ${old} → ${now}.`)
  }
  if(item==='fatigue'){
   if(target!=='player'||!Number.isFinite(who.fatigue)||who.fatigue<=0)return state;
   const old=who.fatigue,now=Math.max(0,old-5);
   return D({...state,player:{...who,fatigue:now}},`당신이 ${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[item]}을(를) 사용했다. 피로 ${old} → ${now}.`)
  }
  if(item.startsWith('trait:')){
   if(target!=='wife'||!KO299_TRAITS.some(([id])=>item===`trait:${id}`))return state;
   const id=item.slice(6),next=H(state,w=>({...w,latentTraits:[...new Set([...(w.latentTraits??[]),id])],noticedLatentTraits:[...new Set([...(w.noticedLatentTraits??[]),id])]}));
   // No success/failure signal: a duplicate dose still consumes stock, and
   // Ownership is never used as a gate; either path reveals the taken trait.
   return D(next,`${who.name}이(가) ${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[item]}을(를) 복용했다.`)
  }
  const [sign,key]=item.split(':'),delta=sign==='plus'?1:-1,old=who.attributes?.[key];
  if(!Number.isFinite(old)||old+delta<0||old+delta>100)return state;
  const updated={...who,attributes:{...who.attributes,[key]:old+delta}},next=target==='wife'?{...state,characters:{...state.characters,[state.primaryCharacterId]:updated}}:{...state,player:updated};
  return D(next,`${target==='wife'?who.name:'당신'}이(가) ${KO299_ITEM_NAMES[item]}을(를) 사용했다. ${KO299_ATTRS.find(x=>x[0]===key)[1]} ${old} → ${old+delta}.`)
 }
 return state;
}

function koShopData299(md,action,before,after){
 const pending=koPendingGuestBaselines299;koPendingGuestBaselines299={};
